We have assigned individual alphabets to the seven planets from the numbers remaining after the twelve signs. And note: when a letter is doubled in speech, its number is not written twice, but once, and it is marked with a point above.

The sixth

--- Page 619 ---

610

With God’s mercy, we have brought the sixth and last book of our Polygraphia to its close; in it, as we conclude our discourse with the wandering stars, we give the reader certain points of advance warning. First, whenever he uses the alphabet of any sign or planet, he should prudently add its character or that same sign, for the sake of a double advantage. By this, excessive labor is removed from the friendly reader, and the mystery will remain secret, while anyone ignorant of the art will think that a table written in numbers pertains to signs or planets, and will suspect no hidden mystery at all in the letters. But we have adopted letters from a distance, so that suspicion is not to be aroused among writers; for a clever reader, when he is touched by suspicion of something hidden, if he discovers the function of one number, will easily compute the whole alphabet.

Second, whoever desires to be a keen practitioner of this matter, remember that, as often happens, a doubled letter should be written only once and marked above with a point. For example, I wish to write Ioannes from the alphabet of Aries: I do not double 18, which is n, but write thus: 14. 16. 7. 18. 10. 23. Or abbas: 6. 7. 6. 23. In the case of Ioannes, abbas, one must proceed with similar caution; for unless the practitioner is endowed with skill and knows how to find greater things from small ones,

[segment 129]
--- Page 620 ---

greater things from small, he will not be a fit person to whom these slight matters of ours ought to be disclosed. Not everything is to be communicated indiscriminately to everyone, especially things whose use can be turned to good or evil. Moved reasonably by this consideration, we have openly handed down many things in these six books of the Polygraphy to those who wish to know; but we have allowed many more secrets to lie hidden beneath what is manifest. Finally, we have written a key that explains the whole work, showing how it is to be understood in each metaphor. Yet, by the counsel of certain men, we have ordered it to remain hidden at home, so that students may not lack material for exercising the memory, and that the wicked may be deprived of the occasion for knowing things which they are not willing to use well. In individual passages many mysteries lie hidden; and the greater the things you discover from these, the greater still are those that remain to be discovered. A way is prepared for beginners, in which it will not be difficult for those who advance, in the course of time, to discover many things, rare and very great. Here one who did not know how will learn to write in Latin, and prudently to wrap all the mysteries of his will in a foreign speech. But that an unlearned person may be able not only to understand his own mysteries which he has wrapped up, but also openly to set forth the matter in a language of which until then he had always been ignorant, is a task of no great labor, if the paraphrast has fully grasped our intention in this volume. For beneath the bark lie the kernels of our Steganography, buried with just reason, lest they be exposed to unworthy babblers. Finally, it was worth the effort that one work should not prejudice the other,

--- Page 621 ---

but that its own right should be distributed to each inviolate. Wearied after a long voyage, here in this sea, under the name of Jesus Christ, the Savior of the faithful, we cast anchor on the twenty-first day of the month of March, in the year of the Lord’s birth 1508, in the eleventh indiction of the Romans, at the city of the eastern Franks which they call both Würzburg and Herbipolis.

FINIS.

[ornamental cipher]

--- Page 622 ---

A Booklet of Johannes Trithemius, Abbot of Spanheim, etc., on the Seven Secondary Powers, that is, the intelligences or spirits moving the spheres after God, containing within itself many secrets worthy to be known, in marvelous brevity.

Dedicated to the Emperor, the divine Maximilian Augustus, pious and wise; copied from the archetype in the year 1545 and published anew.

In the Year of Christ 1600.

--- Page 623 ---

A Booklet of Johannes Trithemius, Abbot of Spanheim, on the heavenly intelligences governing the spheres after God.

It is the opinion of very many of the ancients, most wise Caesar, that this lower world is governed, by the ordinance of the first intellect, which is God, through secondary intelligences. Assenting to their opinion, the Conciliator of the Physicians says that seven spirits were set over the seven planets from the beginning of heaven and earth. Each one of these governs the world for 354 years and, in order, four months. Very many most learned men have hitherto given assent to this position. I make it known to Your Most Sacred Majesty only by reporting it, not by asserting it.

--- Page 624 ---

The first angel, or spirit of Saturn, is called Orifiel. God entrusted the world to him to be governed from the beginning of creation, and his rule began on the fifteenth day of the month of March, in the first year of the world, and lasted 354 years and four months. Orifiel, however, is a name of office, not of nature, attributed to the spirit by reason of his action. Under his rule human beings were rude and rustic, dwelling in solitude like beasts. This needs no proof from me, since it is manifest to all from the text of Genesis.

The second governor of the world is Anael, the spirit of Venus. After Orifiel he began to rule the influence of his planet in the year of the world 354, in the fourth month, that is, on the twenty-fourth day of the month of June, and he governed the world for 354 years and four months, up to the 780th year of the created world, as is clear to one who calculates the period. Under this rule of Anael, human beings began to be more cultivated, to establish houses and cities, to discover manual arts, weaving, tent-making, and things similar to these; also to indulge more fully in the pleasures of the flesh, to take beautiful wives for themselves, to forget God, to depart in many things from natural simplicity, to invent games and songs, to play the cithara, and to devise whatever pertains to the order and worship of Venus. And this wantonness of life endured among human beings until the flood, taking up the proof of its own depravity.

The third

[segment 130]
--- Page 625 ---

2 *On the Secondary Intelligences*

Zachariel.

Third, Zachariel, the angel of Jupiter, began to govern the world in the year of the creation of heaven and earth 780, month 8, that is, on the 26th day of the month of October, and he governed the world for 354 years and four months, up to the year of the created world 1063 inclusive. Under his direction human beings first began to claim lordship over one another, to practice hunting, to make tents, and to adorn their bodies with various garments. A great division arose between the good and the wicked: the good calling upon God, as did Enoch, whom the Lord took away; the wicked, however, running after the enticements of the flesh. Under this Zachariel's rule human beings also began to live more civilly, to endure the laws of their elders, and to grow tame from their former wildness. Under his leadership too Adam, the first man, died, leaving to all posterity the testament of necessary death. Finally, at this time various inventions and arts of human beings emerged, as the historiographers have set forth more clearly.

Raphael.

Fourth, the ruler of the world was Raphael, the spirit of Mercury, who began in the year of the creation of heaven and earth 1063, on the 24th day of February, and presided for 354 years and four months; and his dominion lasted up to the year of the world 1417 and the fourth month. In these times writing was first invented, and letters were first devised in the likeness or form of trees and plants; yet afterwards, with time and cultivation, they took on a more refined ornament, and the peoples changed the appearance of the characters according to their own judgment.

--- Page 626 ---

*Booklet.* 3

The use of musical instruments also began to multiply under the rule of this Raphael, and the exchange of merchandise was first invented among human beings. The rude boldness of navigation too was first ventured in these times, and many other things of this kind.

Samael.

Fifth, the governor of the world was Samael, the angel of Mars, who began on the 26th day of the month of June, in the year of the world 1417. He governed the world for 354 years and 4 months, up to the year 1771, month 8. Under his rule human beings imitated the nature of Mars. Likewise, under this Samael's direction, the universal flood occurred in the year of the world 1656, as is clearly evident from the history of Genesis. And it should be noted that the ancient philosophers handed down that whenever Samael, the spirit of Mars, is ruler of the world, a notable alteration of monarchy arises. Religions and sects vary, laws are changed, and principalities and kingdoms are transferred to outsiders, as we can easily find in the histories in order. But Samael does not manifest this disposition of his character immediately from the beginning of his rule, but after he has passed the midpoint of his governance. The same is to be understood of the spirits of the other planets, as is clear in the histories: they all, according to the properties of the nature of their stars, influence and act upon the lower things of this world.

Gabriel.

Sixth, the governor of the world was Gabriel, the angel of the Moon, who began after Samael of Mars on the 28th day of the month

--- Page 627 ---

4 *On the Secondary Intelligences*

of October, in the year of the world 1771, month 8, and governed the world for 354 years and 4 months, up to the year of the world 2126. In these times human beings were again multiplied, and they founded various cities. And it should be noted that the Hebrews say the flood occurred in the year of the world 1656, under the rule of Mars. But the Seventy interpreters, Isidore, and Bede confirm that it occurred in the year 2242, under the governance of the spirit of the Moon, Gabriel; and this seems to me more consonant with the truth for a reason which it is not the present time to set out.

Michael.

Seventh, the governor of the world was Michael, the angel of the Sun, who began on the 24th of February, in the year of the world, according to the common reckoning, 2126, and he governed the world for 354 years and four months, up to the year of the age of the world 2480, month 4. Under the rule of the angel of the Sun, just as the histories agree with the truth, kings first began to exist among mortals; among them Nimrod was the first who, through lust for domination, seized tyrannical command over his companions. The worship of gods too was instituted by human foolishness, and weak men began to adore their princes as gods. Various arts were also invented by human beings at this time: namely mathematics, astronomy, and magic; and worship of the one God began to be offered to different created things, and knowledge of the true God gradually fell into oblivion through human superstition. In these times too architecture was invented, and human beings began to use more refined customs and institutions.

The origin of kings. The source of idolatry.

Eighth

[segment 131]
--- Page 628 ---

In the eighth order thereafter, Orifiel, the angel of Saturn, again began to govern the world on the twenty-sixth day of the month of June, in the year from the beginning of the world 2480 and four months; and for 354 years and four months he governed the world a second time, up to the year of the world 2834 and the eighth month. Under the governance of this angel, peoples were multiplied, the earth was divided into regions, many kingdoms were established, the Tower of Babel was built, the confusion of languages occurred, and human beings were scattered over the whole earth. People also began to cultivate the earth more carefully, to establish fields, sow grain, plant vineyards, set trees in the ground, and acquire more diligently whatever pertains to food and clothing. From that time distinction of nobility first began to arise among human beings, when men surpassing the rest in character and ability received marks of honor from the great ones of the earth according to their merits. From then on the whole world first began to come into human knowledge, since, as peoples multiplied everywhere, many kingdoms were arising and various differences of languages followed.

In the ninth order, Anael, the spirit of Venus, again began to govern the world on the twenty-ninth day of October, in the year of the creation of heaven and earth 2834, in the eighth month; and he presided for 354 years and four months, up to the year of the world 3189. In these times human beings, delivering the true God to oblivion, began to worship and honor the dead and their statues as God; this error occupied the world for more than two thousand years. Introducing

--- Page 629 ---

curious bodily adornments and various kinds of musical instruments, human beings again pursued lust and the pleasure of the flesh too eagerly, and in these times they also set up and dedicated statues and temples to their gods. Incantations and evil arts were devised in those times by Zoroaster, first king of the Bactrians, and by various others; Ninus, king of the Assyrians, defeated him in war.

In the tenth order, Zachariel, the angel of Jupiter, again began to govern on the last day of the month of February, in the year of the founding of heaven and earth 3189; and he governed, as usual, for 354 years and four months, up to the year of the world itself 3543 and the fourth month. These were joyful times, which could truly be called golden, in which the abundance of all things both brought increase to the human race and gave the world its greatest adornment. Likewise at this time God gave Abraham the law of circumcision and first promised the restoration of human salvation through the incarnation of his only-begotten Son. Under the rule of this spirit, the patriarchs, founders of justice, shone forth, and the just were divided from the impious by their own will and work. In these times Jupiter also flourished in Arcadia, called Lisanias of Heaven and Son of God, a king who first gave laws to the Arcadians, made them much more civilized in their customs, taught the worship of God, built temples, instituted priests, and procured many useful things for the human race; for these reasons he was called Jupiter by them, and after death was regarded as a god. Now he derived

--- Page 630 ---

his origin from the sons of Heber, namely from Jerah, as the histories relate. Prometheus also, son of Atlas, under the rule of this angel, was said to have made human beings, because from rude people he made them learned, humane, gentle, and polished in manners and knowledge. By art he made images move of themselves. He first devised the use of the ring; likewise he invented the scepter, the diadem, and royal insignia. Other Jovian persons also flourished in these times, very wise men and women, who by their ability handed down many useful inventions to the human race; and because of the greatness of their wisdom, when dead they were thought to have been numbered among the gods. These were, namely, Phoroneus, who first established laws and judgments among the Greeks; Sol, Minerva, Ceres, Serapis among the Egyptians, and many others.

In the eleventh order, Raphael, the spirit of Mercury, again took up the government of the world on the first day of the month of July, in the year of the world 3543 and the fourth month; and he presided for 354 years and four months, up to the year of the creation of heaven and earth 3897 and the eighth month. In these times, as is clearly evident from the histories of the ancients, human beings devoted themselves more to wisdom. Among them were the last very famous figures: Mercury, Bacchus, Omogyrus, Isis, Inachus, Argus, Apollo, Cecrops, and many others, who by their inventions benefited both the world then and the later world. Various superstitions also in the worship of idols were established by human beings in these times, and incantations and the arts of diabolical images increased in a marvelous way

[segment 132]
--- Page 631 ---

8 ON THE SEVEN SECONDARY INTELLIGENCES

...in a wonderful manner increased, and whatever subtlety and ingenuity is customarily attributed to Mercury was at that time increased. Moses, the most wise leader of the Hebrews, skilled in many matters and arts, a worshiper of the true and one God, delivered the people of the Hebrews from servitude to the Egyptians into freedom. At this time also Janus first reigned in Italy, after whom came Saturn, who taught that fields were enriched with dung, and was held to be a god. Around this time Cadmus invented Greek letters, and Carmentis, daughter of Evander, Latin letters. Under the rule of this angel Raphael of Mercury, almighty God gave his people the law in writing through Moses, which bears manifest testimony to the future birth of Christ in the flesh. A remarkable diversity of religions also arose in the world. Many Sibyls, prophets, soothsayers, haruspices, magi, and seers flourished in these times, such as the Erythraean, Delphic, and Phrygian Sibyl, together with the others.

In the twelfth order Samael, the angel of Mars, began again to rule the world on the second day of the month of October, in the year of the world 3892, in the eighth month; and his rule lasted 354 years and 4 months, down to the year 4252. Under his rule occurred that great and most famous destruction of Troy in Asia Minor, and there took place a remarkable transformation of monarchy and of many kingdoms, and the foundation anew of many cities, such as Paris, Mainz, Carthage, Naples, and many others. Many kingdoms also newly...

--- Page 632 ---

LITTLE BOOK. 9

...arose, such as those of the Lacedaemonians, Corinthians, Hebrews, and many others. In these times there were very great wars and battles of kings and peoples throughout the whole world, and various changes of empires. From this time the Venetians reckon the beginning both of their people and of their city from the Trojans. And it should be noted that very many other nations, both in Europe and in Asia, claim that they took their origin from the Trojans; to these I have decided to grant only as much credence as they can persuade me of by sufficient testimony to the truth. Frivolous are the things they bring forward about their nobility and antiquity, wishing openly to boast, as though there had been no peoples in Europe before the destruction of Troy, and as though no one among the Trojans themselves had been ignoble.

Likewise, under the dominion of this planet Saul was made the first king of the Jews, and afterward David, whose son, King Solomon, built in Jerusalem the temple of the true God, the most famous in the whole world. From this point the Spirit of God, illuminating his prophets with a fuller light of his grace, caused them to foretell not only the future incarnation of the Lord Savior, but many other things as well, as the sacred histories testify. Among them were Nathan, the prophet of King David, Gad, Asaph, Ahijah, Shemaiah, Azariah, Anan, with many others. Homer, the Greek poet and writer of the destruction of Troy, Dares the Phrygian, and Dictys the Cretan, who were present at that very destruction and likewise described it, are read to have lived in these times.

--- Page 633 ---

10 ON THE SEVEN SECONDARY INTELLIGENCES

In the thirteenth order Gabriel, the spirit of the Moon, again assumed the rule of the world on the thirtieth day of January, in the year from the origin of the universe 4252; and he presided over his dominion for 354 years and 4 months, down to the year of the world 4606 and the fourth month. At that time several prophets flourished among the Hebrews, namely Elijah, Elisha, Micah, Obadiah, and many others. There were various changes in the kingdom of the Hebrews. Lycurgus gave statutes and laws to the Lacedaemonians. Capetus Silvius, Tiberinus Silvius, Romulus Silvius, and Numitor, kings of Italy, flourished under this spirit. Many new kingdoms also took their beginnings under him, such as those of the Lydians, Medes, Spartans, and others. The monarchy of the Assyrians came to an end in Sardanapalus. Likewise the kingdom of the Macedonians was consumed. Various laws were imposed upon human beings, and the worship of the true God was neglected; the religion of false gods was propagated exceedingly.

The city of Rome was built under the dominion of this spirit, in the year 1484, which was the 239th year of the rule of Gabriel himself in the order; and the kingdom of the Silvii of Italy came to an end, and that of the Romans began. In these times also Thales, Solon, Periander, Cleobulus, Bias, and Pittacus, the seven sages, flourished in Greece, and from this point philosophers and poets began to be held in esteem. Romulus, the founder of the city, first ruled at Rome for 37 years, a fratricide and promoter of sedition. After him Numa Pompilius ruled for 42 years; he enlarged the worship of the gods, in the time of Hezekiah, king of Judah. [unclear]

[segment 133]
--- Page 634 ---

*Little Book*

of the Jews, maintained the kingdom in great peace. Near the end of the governance of this spirit of the Moon, Nabuchodonosor, king of Babylon, took Jerusalem, destroyed King Sedechias, and led the whole people away captive. The prophet Hieremias was illustrious; he had foretold this destruction, and likewise the future liberation from Babylon.

After Gabriel, Michael, the spirit of the Sun, again assumed the fourteenth rule of the world. He began on the first day of the month of May, in the year of the world 4606, in the fourth month, and ruled the world in his order for 354 years, down to the year from the creation of the world 4960 and eight months. In the time of this rule, Evil Merodach, king of Babylon, restored liberty and a king to the people of the Hebrews, under the direction of Michael, who, as Daniel writes, stood for the people of the Jews, who had been entrusted to him by God. Likewise in these times the monarchy of the kingdom of the Persians began; its first king, Darius, and its second, Cyrus, utterly destroyed that most powerful kingdom of Babylon in the days of Balthasar, as Daniel and the prophets had foretold. In these times the Cumaean Sibyl was illustrious. She offered nine books to King Tarquinius Priscus to be bought for a price; in them was contained the course of future warnings and the ordering of the whole republic of the Romans. But when the king refused the price, the Sibyl burned the first three books while he looked on, and demanded the same price for the remaining six. When he again refused it, she also gave three others to the fire to be burned.

--- Page 635 ---

She would have done the same with the last books, had the king not been persuaded by the counsel of his men and redeemed them from destruction by paying the price that had been demanded for all of them. Likewise, the Romans abolished kings and established two consuls each year. Phalaris the tyrant occupied Sicily in those times. Magic also was held in great esteem among the kings of the Persians at that time. Pythagoras the philosopher and many others then flourished among the Greeks. The temple and the city at Jerusalem were rebuilt. Esdras the prophet, using memory in place of the archetype, restored the books of Moses burned by the Chaldaeans, who were also called Babylonians. Xerxes, king of the Persians, led an army against the Greeks, but accomplished little. Rome was taken, burned, and destroyed by the Gauls, except only the Capitol, which was protected by a goose that roused the weary fighters. The very famous wars of the Athenians also occurred in these times. Socrates and Plato, the philosophers, lived then. The Romans again abolished the consuls and instituted tribunes and aediles, and in these times they were involved in many disasters. Alexander the Great also, after the rule of Michael was completed, reigned in Macedonia. He destroyed the monarchy of the Persians under Darius and subjected all Asia, with part of Europe, to his rule. He lived thirty-three years and ruled for twelve years and five months. After his death, countless wars followed, and many evils, and the monarchy was divided into four parts. Among the Hebrews, contention began over the high priesthood. The kingdom of Syria begins.

--- Page 636 ---

*Little Book*

After Michael, in the fifteenth order, Orifiel, the spirit of Saturn, now for the third time assumed the rule of the world, on the last day of the month of September, in the year from the creation of the universe 4960 and eight months, and he presided for 354 years and four months, down to the year of the world 5315. Under his rule the Punic War began between the Romans and the Carthaginians. The city of Rome was almost entirely consumed by fire and water. The Colossus, a cast bronze image 126 feet high, fell in an earthquake. In this one year there was peace for the Romans after the Punic War; for 440 years they had never been without war. Jerusalem, together with the temple, was burned and destroyed by Antiochus Epiphanes. The history and wars of the Maccabees took place. In these times, in the 606th year from the founding of the city, Carthage was destroyed and burned for seventeen continuous days. In Sicily there was a conspiracy of 70,000 slaves against their masters. Great portents were seen in Europe in those times: domestic animals fled to the woods, blood flowed, and a fiery globe flashed from heaven with a crash. Mithridates, king of Pontus and Armenia, waged war with the Romans for forty years. The kingdom of the Hebrews was restored, having been interrupted for 575 years, from the time of Zedechias down to Aristobolus. Likewise the German Teutons invaded the Romans and, after many battles, are said to have been defeated; 160,000 were killed, apart from countless others who killed themselves with their households, under the consuls Caius and Manlius. Nevertheless, many Romans had previously been destroyed by them. Finally, wars...

[segment 134]
--- Page 637 ---

14 ON THE SECONDARY POWERS

The civil wars, which lasted forty years, greatly wore down the Romans. Three suns appeared at Rome, and not for long, but soon they were reduced into one. A few years later, Gaius Julius Caesar seized the rule of the Romans; after him Octavian Augustus enlarged it and united Asia, Africa, and Europe into one monarchy. He ruled for thirty-six years, and through him God bestowed peace on the whole world.

In the year 751 from the founding of the city, the forty-second year of Octavian Caesar Augustus, in the 245th year and eighth month of the prescribed rule of Orifiel, spirit of Saturn, on the twenty-fifth day of the month of December, JESUS CHRIST, the Son of God, was born of the Virgin Mary in Bethlehem of Judea.

Notice how beautiful is the ordering of divine providence. For the world was first created under the rule of Orifiel, and under his third rule it was also mercifully redeemed, restored, and renewed, so that such harmony of events seems to lend no small credibility to this account of the governance of the world by the seven planetary spirits. For in the first rule of Orifiel there was one monarchy over the whole world; under the second, as we said above, it was divided into many. Again under the third, as is seen, it was called back into unity, although, if we measure rightly, it is also clear that in the second rule of Orifiel there was one monarchy of the whole world, when the tower was being built at Babylon. From this point the kingdom of the Jews was taken away; the victims and the continual sacrifice ceased; nor will freedom be restored to the Jews before the third revolution

--- Page 638 ---

LITTLE BOOK

15 of Michael the spirit; and this will happen after the birth of Christ in the year 1880, in the eighth month, that is, in the year of the world 7170, in the eighth month.

In those times many of the Jews and Gentiles took up the Christian religion, while the very simple and rustic preached it, men whom no human instruction, but the Spirit of God, had enlightened. Then the world began to be called back to the first innocence of simplicity, with Orifiel, the spirit of Saturn, governing it in each case. Heavenly things were mingled with earthly things. Many Christians also were cruelly killed by the rulers of the world for the faith that they preached. Around the end of Orifiel’s rule, Jerusalem was destroyed by the Romans, and the Jews were scattered into every land; 1,100,000 were killed and 80,000 sold. The rest fled, and thus the Romans utterly destroyed Judea.

[unclear]

Sixteenth in order, after Orifiel, Anael, the spirit of Venus, resumed the rule of the whole world for the third time, on the last day of January, in the year 5315 from the creation of heaven and earth, but in the year 109 from the birth of Christ. He governed the world for 354 years and four months, until the year of the world 5669 and four months, but until the end of the 463rd year of the Lord’s Nativity. And it should be noted that through almost the whole period of this rule of the angel of Venus, the Church of the Christians flourished and prevailed amid persecutions, countless thousands of human beings being killed for the faith of Christ. Finally, in these times several heresies began to spring up in the Church, and not without the labor and blood of good men.

[segment 135]
--- Page 639 ---

16 ON THE SECONDS

...of men, were in time extinguished. Many men flourished in those times, most learned and most eloquent in every kind of knowledge: theologians, astronomers, physicians, orators, historiographers, and others like them, not only among the gentiles but also among Christians. At last the persecution of the unbelievers against the Church ceased, after Constantine Caesar the Great accepted the faith, in the year of the world 5539, after the midpoint of the rule of Anael himself, the angel of Venus. Although afterwards it was several times disturbed in part by the impious, nevertheless it remained for a long time entirely very quiet. From then on the human race, which from the time of King Ninus had wandered miserably for 2300 years in the worship of idols, was mercifully recalled to knowledge of the one God.

The various arts of subtlety were increased in these times, and through what was fitting to the nature of Venus they took on adornment and growth. For human customs change with time, and lower bodies are disposed according to the influence of the higher. The mind indeed is free, and does not receive the influence of the stars, unless, by bending itself too much toward the commerce it has with the body, it stains its own affection. For the angels who are the movers of the orbs destroy or overturn nothing that nature has established. A comet of unusual size preceded the death of Constantine. The Arian heresy disturbed the holy Church in many places at the end of this rule. In the time of Julian Caesar, crosses appeared on people’s linen garments.

[Marginal notes: Constantine the Great becomes Christian. Comet. Arian heresy. Crosses on garments.]

--- Page 640 ---

LITTLE BOOK. 17

In Asia and Palestine there followed wars, pestilences, and famines in those places where the crosses had appeared. In these times also, around the year of the Lord 360, the Franks took their beginning in Germany; later, after they devastated Gaul, they named the conquered land Francia after their own name. The description of Francia was great in length and breadth; its metropolis was Mainz, but now Würzburg alone. The Bavarians, the Rhinelanders, the Saxons, the Thuringians, and the surrounding bishoprics today occupy a great part of Francia in Germany.

Likewise, in the 280th year of the government of this Anael, the Roman Empire began to decline, when the city was captured and burned by the Goths, the imperial seat having first been transferred under Constantine into Greece; this was the beginning and cause of the whole decline of the monarchy. For around the end of this rule of Anael there arose Radagaisus and Athaulf, kings of the Goths; likewise afterwards Genseric of the Vandals, and also Attila of the Huns, who wretchedly tore the empire apart, as is clear in these histories.

[unclear]

After Anael, in the seventeenth order, Zachariel, spirit of Jupiter, resumed the universal government for the third time on the first day of June, in the year of the world 5669, the fourth month; but of the Lord’s Nativity 463, the seventh month. He presided for 354 years and four months, until the year of the world 6023 and the eighth month, but of the Lord 817. In these times many, out of love for Christian philosophy, withdrew to the desert; many portents also appeared,

[Marginal notes: Origin or beginnings of the German Franks. Mainz formerly the metropolis of the Franks. Decline of the Roman Empire. Invasions of the Goths, Vandals, and Huns. Comet.]

[segment 136]
--- Page 641 ---

18. On the Seven Secondary Intelligences

Comets, earthquakes, rains of blood. Merlin, born in Tumbe, foretold marvelous things at the beginning of this rule. Arcturus, whom people commonly call Arcus, the most famous king of Britain, conquered the barbarians, restored peace to the Church, fought many battles victoriously, enlarged the faith of Christ, and subjected all Gaul, Norway, Dacia, and many provinces to his empire. He was the most glorious of all the kings of his time; after he had accomplished many outstanding deeds, he appeared nowhere, though the Britons waited for many years for him to return. Concerning him, minstrels once produced marvelous songs. In his reign England was in flower, and thirty kingdoms served him.

In these times the orders of monks in the Church of God began to multiply. Theodoric, the Arian king of the Goths, possessed all Italy and killed the consul Boethius. Everything was full of disturbance, both the empire and the Church. In the East the emperors Zeno and Anastasius were Arians; in Italy Theodoric and his successors, and in Africa Honorius, king of the Vandals, exercised no small tyranny. At last Clovis, king of the Franks in Gaul, became a Christian, overcame the Goths, and imposed peace on affairs, though not everywhere in the world, in the time of St. Benedict, in the five-hundredth year of the Christians, or thereabouts, at the beginning of the rule of this angel Zachariel of Jupiter. It is proper to his spirit to change empires and kingdoms; histories show in many ways that this happened in this revolution, and that Raphael the angel could not do it himself.

--- Page 642 ---

Booklet. 23

It was almost submerged by the waves of the sea, and more than 100,000 people perished. The Tartars laid waste Hungary and Poland, after first overcoming Greater Armenia and many regions. In the year of the Christians 1244, a certain Jew at Toledo, while digging, found a book in which it was written: “In the third world Christ will be born of the Virgin Mary, and will suffer for the salvation of human beings.” He soon believed and was baptized. The “third world” was the third revolution of the spirit of Saturn, of which it was spoken above; at its beginning Christ was born of the Virgin.

The Roman pontiffs, depriving Frederick of the empire, said that it had been vacant for twenty-eight years. [unclear] William, count of Holland; Conrad, son of Frederick; Alfonso, king of Castile; and Richard, count of Cornwall, brother of the king of England; and evils were multiplied on earth.

At this time, around the year of the Lord 1260, the confederation of the Swiss first began: a people small in number, which grew with time and killed many nobles, driving others from their borders. They are warlike men, whose commonwealth is known to all the peoples of Germany. In the year of the Christians 1273, Rudolph of Habsburg was made emperor by the election of the princes, and he ruled for eighteen years: a man prudent and excellent in every respect, from whom all the later dukes of Austria descended. The assault of the Tartars.

[segment 137]
--- Page 643 ---

24 ON THE SEVEN SECONDARIES

The Tartars, invading the lands of the Christians, Constantinople, and Greece, inflicted much damage on the Christians. The Saracens seized many cities in Asia and killed more than four hundred times a thousand Christians. After Rudolph died, Adolf of Nassau was elected king and ruled for six years; Albert, Rudolph’s son, later defeated him in battle near Worms and killed him, and was elected emperor in the year 1298. He ruled ten years and was killed by his brother’s son. The Order of the Templars was destroyed by order of Pope Clement V. The island of Rhodes was taken by the Christians from the hands of the Saracens, in the fourth year of the siege and continuous war against them. After Albert was killed by his nephew, Henry VIII, Count of Luxembourg, was made emperor; he ruled five years. When he died, Louis IV of Bavaria ruled for thirty-two years, beginning in the year 1315; the Roman pontiffs gave him the crown. Frederick, Duke of Austria, opposed him, but was defeated by him. After Louis, Charles IV, King of Bohemia, was made emperor; he raised the bishopric of Prague to an archbishopric and ruled thirty-one years. There were very great earthquakes. This Charles newly instituted many things concerning tolls in favor of the prince-electors. Gunther, Count of Schwarzburg, calling himself king, opposed Emperor Charles, but he had no success against him. After Charles, his son Wenceslaus ruled twenty-two years; after him Jodocus, Margrave of Moravia, succeeded Sigismund,

--- Page 644 ---

LIBELLUS. 25

brother of Wenceslaus. Wenceslaus was deposed. Leopold, Duke of Austria, while fighting in battle against the Swiss, was killed together with eight counts and more than four thousand men. Under Wenceslaus, King of the Bohemians and emperor, the doctrine of the Hussites had its beginning. After he was deposed, Rupert, Count Palatine of the Rhine and Duke of Bavaria, was elected, and ruled ten years. In the year of the Lord 1369 the Christians brought war upon the Saracens, but it ended badly because of the arrogance of the French, since more than 100,000 of our men fell, besides the captives, among whom was John, Duke of Burgundy. There were many wars in these times. In the year 1407 Sigismund became emperor for twenty-seven years. He tried to devastate Bohemia in order to extirpate heresies, but made little progress. The kingdom of France was cruelly devastated and afflicted by the English and the Burgundians. When Sigismund had departed this life, Albert, Duke of Austria, his son-in-law, succeeded him in the year of the Christians 1438, and ruled only two years, an excellent man and worthy of empire. When he died, Frederick III, Duke of Austria, his son, was made emperor by the election of the princes, and ruled fifty-six years, a man divine in intellect and peaceful in spirit. He began to reign in the year of the Lord 1440. In the year of the Christians 1453 Constantinople was taken by the Turks through the betrayal of a certain Genoese, and gradually all Greece fell away from the Christian faith. Moreover, in his time many Christian kingdoms and provinces were devastated and seized by the Turks. The Christians waged many wars

[segment 138]
--- Page 645 ---

26 ON THE SEVEN SECONDARY INTELLIGENCES

Many wars.

At this time they waged very grave wars among themselves in Gaul, England, Westphalia, Prussia, Flanders, Swabia, and other places. In these times the art of printing was newly invented at Mainz, the metropolis of Germany, by marvelous industry, as a gift of divine favor. In the year of Christians 1456, the Turks were overthrown in Hungary, and many of the faithless perished. The pilgrimage of boys to Saint Michael was remarkable. There were earthquakes in the kingdom of Naples, and more than 40,000 people perished. In the year of the Lord 1462, Mainz, the metropolis of the Franks, was captured and plundered. Charles, duke of Burgundy, defeated the French in the year 1465. Later, in the year 67, he destroyed Dinant and Liege. In the year 1473 he entered Guelders and powerfully took possession of it, and likewise the whole duchy of Lorraine. A comet appeared throughout the month of January in the year of the Lord 1472. Charles, duke of Burgundy, afterward besieged the town of Neuss for the space of a year, in the year 74; later, in the year 1477, he was killed in battle, a great-hearted prince. In these times the Turks captured many cities of the Christians: Negroponte in Euboea, also the kingdom of Bosnia, the duchy of Spetis, Achaea, Mitylene, and many kingdoms in the East. In the year 1476 a gathering of fools, full of errors, took place in German Franconia at Niklashausen. In the year 1480 the Turks besieged the Rhodians with a great army, but they did not prevail. In the same year, departing from Rhodes, they captured Otranto, with more than 12,000 Christians killed.

--- Page 646 ---

LIBELLUS. 27

Only 22 escaped by flight. And in the following year Mehmed, emperor of the Turks, died; Bayezid, his firstborn, succeeded him in the kingdom, now ruling in his twenty-seventh year. In the year of Christians 1486, Maximilian, son of Frederick, was made King of the Romans at Frankfurt, and in the year 1508 was saluted as Caesar by Pope Julius. He founded the order of the knighthood of Saint George against heretics and Turks; he subdued the Swiss in war; he has attacked the rebellious Sicambri in war down to this day, and he will be fortunate against all treaty-breakers. The king of the French, in his usual fashion a persecutor of the empire, has been found to be contriving new plots. May the Almighty protect those assigned to the rule of Samael in the year 1508. The Venetians, rebels against the rule of Caesar, have been marked by ban and war. There will be punishment for obstinacy, and a reward for considered satisfaction.

Notable.

Around the end of this third revolution of Samael, the image of alteration will pass over to the first, and there will be the destruction of many. For unless T., with God ministering, is brought back to Algos, there will be the transfer of some monarchy, or of some great kingdom. A great sect of religion will arise, the destruction of old religions. It is to be feared that the fourth beast may lose one head. In Samael, Mars in the first place foretold the flood; in the second, the destruction of Troy; in the third, around the end, there will be a great loss of unity. From what has gone before, therefore, let the future things that follow be judged. This third revolution of Mars will not be completed without prophecy and without the institution of some new religion. From this point, the year of Christians 1508.

[segment 139]
--- Page 647 ---

28 ON THE SEVEN SECONDARIES, BOOKLET.

1508. There remain 17 years until the end of the rule of Samael. In these years there will be signs indicating the beginning of the figure of evils. For in the year of the Christians 1525, the crosses seen on men’s garments a decade earlier, which has now passed, will show their effect. But in the thirteenth year from now, lawfully summoned, you will yield place to one who does not know; greater after death you will rise again for me in the third, unless perhaps a mist conceals you.

In the twentieth order Gabriel, angel of the Moon, will receive the government of the world in the year of the world 6732, in the fourth month, on the fourth day of the month of June, in the year of the Christians 1525; and he will govern the world for 354 years and 4 months, until the year of the world 7086 and the eighth month, and, from the Lord’s nativity, likewise to 1879 years and 11 months. The future sequence of this revolution requires prophecy.

I have not written these things assertively, most sacred Caesar, as matters to which, to the injury of the Orthodox faith, credence should in any way be given. There are, however, some who have reckoned lunar months in these matters; if you think they should be agreed with, those things which I have written otherwise must be changed. With my own hand also I protest, and with my mouth I confess, that in all these matters I believe nothing and admit nothing except what the Catholic Church believes; the rest I reject and despise as vain, fabricated, and superstitious.

Praise be to almighty God.

END.
